{"parentUuid":"06483db1-f8b4-492e-a6c8-754d950c0e3e","isSidechain":false,"message":{"content":[{"id":"call_15076c3e307d4292a0958617","input":{"file_path":"/home/admin/Downloads/biaozhul/opp14/b/src/test/java/com/pushdown/TestRunner.java","content":"package com.pushdown;\n\nimport com.pushdown.api.JsonPlan;\nimport com.pushdown.api.Server;\nimport com.pushdown.exec.Engine;\nimport com.pushdown.expr.Expr;\nimport com.pushdown.json.Json;\nimport com.pushdown.opt.Optimizer;\nimport com.pushdown.plan.Plan;\nimport com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer;\nimport java.net.URI;\nimport java.net.http.HttpClient;\nimport java.net.http.HttpRequest;\nimport java.net.http.HttpResponse;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.LinkedHashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.Set;\nimport java.util.TreeSet;\n\n/**\n * Test runner (plain main, no test framework). Exits non-zero on any failure.\n *\n * Includes the acceptance exhaustive check: enumerate all small tables over\n * the domain {NULL,1,2} (every subset of up to 3 rows of the 9 possible\n * 2-column rows), cross them, and for INNER and LEFT joins compare the\n * original plan result against the rewritten plan result for a battery of\n * predicates covering right-table NULL filters, duplicate column names and\n * constant-false predicates.\n */\npublic final class TestRunner {\n\n    static int passed = 0, failed = 0;\n    static final List<String> failures = new ArrayList<>();\n\n    static void check(boolean cond, String msg) {\n        if (cond) passed++;\n        else {\n            failed++;\n            failures.add(msg);\n            System.out.println(\"FAIL: \" + msg);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {\n        testJson();\n        testEval();\n        testNullRejection();\n        testPushdownStructure();\n        testExhaustive();\n        testHttp();\n        System.out.printf(\"%npassed=%d failed=%d%n\", passed, failed);\n        if (failed > 0) {\n            System.out.println(\"failures:\");\n            failures.forEach(f -> System.out.println(\"  - \" + f));\n            System.exit(1);\n        }\n        System.out.println(\"ALL TESTS PASSED\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ JSON\n\n    static void testJson() {\n        String s = \"{\\\"a\\\":[1,2.5,null,\\\"x\\\"],\\\"b\\\":{\\\"c\\\":true}}\";\n        Object v = Json.parse(s);\n        check(Json.write(v).equals(s), \"json round trip: \" + Json.write(v));\n        Object n = Json.parse(\"{\\\"lit\\\":null}\");\n        check(Json.write(n).equals(\"{\\\"lit\\\":null}\"), \"json null round trip\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ eval\n\n    static void testEval() {\n        List<Expr.Col> schema = List.of(new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"));\n        Object[] rowNull = {null};\n        Object[] rowOne = {1L};\n\n        Expr eq1 = new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"), new Expr.Lit(1L));\n        check(Expr.eval(eq1, schema, rowNull) == null, \"1 = NULL is UNKNOWN\");\n        check(Boolean.TRUE.equals(Expr.eval(eq1, schema, rowOne)), \"1 = 1 is TRUE\");\n\n        Expr and = new Expr.And(new Expr.Lit(false), new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"));\n        check(Boolean.FALSE.equals(Expr.eval(and, schema, rowNull)), \"FALSE AND NULL is FALSE\");\n\n        Expr or = new Expr.Or(new Expr.Lit(true), new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"));\n        check(Boolean.TRUE.equals(Expr.eval(or, schema, rowNull)), \"TRUE OR NULL is TRUE\");\n\n        Expr isNull = new Expr.IsNull(new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"));\n        check(Boolean.TRUE.equals(Expr.eval(isNull, schema, rowNull)), \"NULL IS NULL is TRUE\");\n\n        Expr notNull = new Expr.Not(new Expr.IsNull(new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\")));\n        check(Boolean.FALSE.equals(Expr.eval(notNull, schema, rowNull)), \"NOT(NULL IS NULL) is FALSE\");\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------- NULL-rejection\n\n    static void testNullRejection() {\n        Set<Expr.Col> right = Set.of(new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"));\n        Expr.Col uc = new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\");\n        check(Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, new Expr.Lit(1L)), right),\n                \"U.C = 1 is null-rejecting\");\n        check(!Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.IsNull(uc), right),\n                \"U.C IS NULL is not null-rejecting\");\n        check(Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.IsNotNull(uc), right),\n                \"U.C IS NOT NULL is null-rejecting\");\n        check(!Optimizer.nullRejecting(\n                new Expr.Or(new Expr.IsNull(uc), new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, new Expr.Lit(1L))), right),\n                \"U.C IS NULL OR U.C = 1 is not null-rejecting\");\n        check(Optimizer.nullRejecting(\n                new Expr.And(new Expr.IsNull(uc), new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, new Expr.Lit(1L))), right),\n                \"U.C IS NULL AND U.C = 1 is null-rejecting (contradiction)\");\n        check(Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.Lit(false), right),\n                \"constant FALSE is (vacuously) null-rejecting\");\n        check(!Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.Lit(true), right),\n                \"constant TRUE is not null-rejecting\");\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------- pushdown structure\n\n    static Map<String, Engine.Table> catalog() {\n        Map<String, Engine.Table> cat = new LinkedHashMap<>();\n        cat.put(\"T\", new Engine.Table(List.of(\"A\", \"B\"), List.of(\n                new Object[]{1L, 1L}, new Object[]{2L, null})));\n        cat.put(\"U\", new Engine.Table(List.of(\"B\", \"C\"), List.of(\n                new Object[]{1L, 1L}, new Object[]{null, null})));\n        return cat;\n    }\n\n    static Plan.Join join(Plan.JoinType type) {\n        return new Plan.Join(type, new Plan.Scan(\"T\"), new Plan.Scan(\"U\"),\n                new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"B\"), new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"B\")));\n    }\n\n    static boolean hasReason(Optimizer.Result r, String rule) {\n        return r.reasons().stream().anyMatch(x -> x.rule().equals(rule));\n    }\n\n    static boolean filterAboveJoin(Plan p) {\n        // true if the top of the (possibly Project-wrapped) plan is a Filter above a Join\n        if (p instanceof Plan.Project pr) return filterAboveJoin(pr.child());\n        return p instanceof Plan.Filter f && stripFilters(f.child()) instanceof Plan.Join;\n    }\n\n    static Plan stripFilters(Plan p) {\n        while (p instanceof Plan.Filter f) p = f.child();\n        return p;\n    }\n\n    static void testPushdownStructure() {\n        Map<String, Engine.Table> cat = catalog();\n\n        // 1. LEFT JOIN + right-side NULL-rejecting predicate -> pushed into right input.\n        Expr ucEq1 = new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"), new Expr.Lit(1L));\n        Plan p1 = new Plan.Filter(ucEq1, join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r1 = Optimizer.optimize(p1, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r1, \"push-right-null-rejecting\"), \"left join: U.C=1 pushed with null-rejecting reason\");\n        check(!filterAboveJoin(r1.plan()), \"left join: U.C=1 no filter left above join\");\n\n        // 2. LEFT JOIN + right-side non-null-rejecting predicate -> stays above.\n        Expr ucIsNull = new Expr.IsNull(new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"));\n        Plan p2 = new Plan.Filter(ucIsNull, join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r2 = Optimizer.optimize(p2, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r2, \"kept-for-null-preservation\"), \"left join: U.C IS NULL kept above with reason\");\n        check(filterAboveJoin(r2.plan()), \"left join: U.C IS NULL filter remains above join\");\n\n        // 3. INNER JOIN + right-side predicate -> pushed.\n        Plan p3 = new Plan.Filter(ucEq1, join(Plan.JoinType.INNER));\n        Optimizer.Result r3 = Optimizer.optimize(p3, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r3, \"push-right-inner\"), \"inner join: U.C=1 pushed right\");\n\n        // 4. LEFT JOIN + left-side predicate -> pushed left.\n        Expr taEq1 = new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"), new Expr.Lit(1L));\n        Plan p4 = new Plan.Filter(taEq1, join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r4 = Optimizer.optimize(p4, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r4, \"push-left\"), \"left join: T.A=1 pushed left\");\n        check(!filterAboveJoin(r4.plan()), \"left join: T.A=1 no filter left above join\");\n\n        // 5. Constant FALSE on LEFT JOIN -> kept above (must not fabricate preserved rows).\n        Plan p5 = new Plan.Filter(new Expr.Lit(false), join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r5 = Optimizer.optimize(p5, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r5, \"constant-kept-above-left-join\"), \"left join: FALSE kept above\");\n        check(filterAboveJoin(r5.plan()), \"left join: FALSE filter remains above join\");\n        check(Engine.execute(r5.plan(), cat).rows().isEmpty(), \"left join: FALSE yields empty result\");\n\n        // 6. Constant FALSE on INNER JOIN -> pushed to left input, result empty.\n        Plan p6 = new Plan.Filter(new Expr.Lit(false), join(Plan.JoinType.INNER));\n        Optimizer.Result r6 = Optimizer.optimize(p6, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r6, \"constant-pushed-inner\"), \"inner join: FALSE pushed\");\n        check(Engine.execute(r6.plan(), cat).rows().isEmpty(), \"inner join: FALSE yields empty result\");\n\n        // 7. Two-side predicate stays above either join type.\n        Expr cross = new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"), new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"));\n        Plan p7 = new Plan.Filter(cross, join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r7 = Optimizer.optimize(p7, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r7, \"kept-above-join\"), \"two-side predicate kept above join\");\n\n        // 8. Constant-folding of 1 = 0.\n        Plan p8 = new Plan.Filter(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Lit(1L), new Expr.Lit(0L)),\n                join(Plan.JoinType.INNER));\n        Optimizer.Result r8 = Optimizer.optimize(p8, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r8, \"constant-fold\"), \"1 = 0 folded to constant\");\n        check(Engine.execute(r8.plan(), cat).rows().isEmpty(), \"1 = 0 yields empty result\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------- exhaustive acceptance\n\n    static List<Expr> predicates() {\n        Expr.Col ta = new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\");\n        Expr.Col uc = new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\");\n        Expr.Lit one = new Expr.Lit(1L);\n        Expr.Lit two = new Expr.Lit(2L);\n        List<Expr> ps = new ArrayList<>();\n        ps.add(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", ta, one));                                   // left only\n        ps.add(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, one));                                   // right, null-rejecting\n        ps.add(new Expr.IsNull(uc));                                          // right, NOT null-rejecting\n        ps.add(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", ta, uc));                                    // both sides\n        ps.add(new Expr.Lit(false));                                          // constant false\n        ps.add(new Expr.And(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", ta, one), new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, one))); // conjunction\n        ps.add(new Expr.IsNotNull(uc));                                       // right, null-rejecting\n        ps.add(new Expr.Or(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, one), new Expr.Bin(\"=\", ta, two))); // both sides\n        ps.add(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", one, new Expr.Lit(0L)));                     // constant false via folding\n        ps.add(new Expr.Or(new Expr.IsNull(uc), new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, one)));// right, NOT null-rejecting\n        return ps;\n    }\n\n    /** All tables with columns (X,Y) over domain {null,1,2}, of size 0..3. */\n    static List<Engine.Table> allSmallTables() {\n        List<Object[]> domain = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Object> vals = Arrays.asList(null, 1L, 2L);\n        for (Object a : vals) for (Object b : vals) domain.add(new Object[]{a, b});\n        List<Engine.Table> tables = new ArrayList<>();\n        int n = domain.size();\n        // all subsets of size 0..3 encoded as bitmasks\n        for (int mask = 0; mask < (1 << n); mask++) {\n            if (Integer.bitCount(mask) > 3) continue;\n            List<Object[]> rows = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) if ((mask & (1 << i)) != 0) rows.add(domain.get(i));\n            tables.add(new Engine.Table(List.of(\"X\", \"Y\"), rows));\n        }\n        return tables;\n    }\n\n    static Plan buildPlan(Expr pred, Plan.JoinType type) {\n        // Duplicate column names on purpose: T.B and U.B.\n        Plan.Join j = new Plan.Join(type, new Plan.Scan(\"T\"), new Plan.Scan(\"U\"),\n                new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"B\"), new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"B\")));\n        Plan.Filter f = new Plan.Filter(pred, j);\n        return new Plan.Project(List.of(\n                new Plan.Item(new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"), \"a\"),\n                new Plan.Item(new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"B\"), \"t_b\"),\n                new Plan.Item(new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"B\"), \"u_b\"),\n                new Plan.Item(new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"), \"c\")), f);\n    }\n\n    static String canonical(Engine.Output out) {\n        TreeSet<String> rows = new TreeSet<>();\n        for (List<Object> r : out.rows()) rows.add(String.valueOf(r));\n        return out.schema() + \" \" + rows;\n    }\n\n    static void testExhaustive() {\n        List<Engine.Table> tables = allSmallTables();\n        List<Expr> preds = predicates();\n        long combos = 0,      }\n    }\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {\n        testJson();\n        testEval();\n        testNullRejection();\n        testPushdownStructure();\n        Expr or = new Expr.Or(new Expr.Lit(true), new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"));\n        check(Boolean.TRUE.equals(Expr.eval(or, schema, rowNull)), \"TRUE OR NULL is TRUE\");\n\n        Expr isNull = --------------------------------------------------------- NULL-rejection\n\n    static void testNullRejection() {\n        Set<Expr.Col> right = Set.of(new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"));\n        Expr.Col uc = new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\");\n        check(Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, new Expr.Lit(1L)), right),\n                \"U.C = 1 is null-rejecting\");\n        check(!Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.IsNull(uc), right),\n                \"U.C IS NULL is not null-rejecting\");\n        check(Optimizer.nullRejecting(new Expr.IsNotNull(uc), right),\n                \"U.C IS NOT NULL is null-rejecting\");\n        check(!Optimizer.nullRejecting(\n                new Expr.Or(new Expr.IsNull(uc), new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, new Expr.Lit(1L))), right),\n plan is a Filter above a Join\n        if (p instanceof Plan.Project pr) return filterAboveJoin(pr.child());\n        return p instanceof Plan.Filter f && stripFilters(f.child()) instanceof LEFT JOIN + right-side NULL-rejecting predicate -> pushed into right input.\n        Expr ucEq1 = new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"), new Expr.Lit(1L));\n        Plan p1 = new Plan.Filter(ucEq1, join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r1 = Optimizer.optimize(p1, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r1, \"push-right-null-rejecting\"), \"left join: U.C=1 pushed with null-rejecting reason\");\n        check(!filterAboveJoin(r1.plan()), \"left join: U.C=1 no filter left above join\");\n\n        // 2. LEFT JOIN + right-side non-null-rejecting predicate -> stays above.\n        Expr ucIsNull = new Expr.IsNull(new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"));\n        Plan p2 = new Plan.Filter(ucIsNull, join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r2 = Optimizer.optimize(p2, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r2, \"kept-for-null-preservation\"), \"left join: U.C IS NULL kept above with reason\");\n        check(filterAboveJoin(r2.plan()), \"left join: U.C IS NULL filter remains above join\");\n\n        // 3. INNER JOIN + right-side predicate -> pushed.\n        Plan p3 = new Plan.Filter(ucEq1, join(Plan.JoinType.INNER));\n        Optimizer.Result r3 = Optimizer.optimize(p3, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r3, \"push-right-inner\"), \"inner join: U.C=1 pushed right\");\n\n        // 4. Two-side predicate stays above either join type.\n        Expr cross = new Expr.Bin(\"=\", new Expr.Col(\"T\", \"A\"), new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\"));\n        Plan p7 = new Plan.Filter(cross, join(Plan.JoinType.LEFT));\n        Optimizer.Result r7 = Optimizer.optimize(p7, cat);\n        check(hasReason(r7, \"kept-above-join\"), \"two-side predicate kept above join\");\n\n        // 8. Expr.Col uc = new Expr.Col(\"U\", \"C\");\n        Expr.Lit one = new Expr.Lit(1L);\n        Expr.Lit two = new Expr.Lit(2L);\n        List<Expr> ps = new ArrayList<>();\n        ps.add(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", ta, one));                                   // left only\n        ps.add(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, one));                                   // right, null-rejecting\n        ps.add(new Expr.IsNull(uc));                                          // right, NOT null-rejecting\n        ps.add(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", ta, uc));                                    // both sides\n        ps.add(new Expr.Lit(false));                                          // constant false\n        ps.add(new Expr.And(new Expr.Bin(\"=\", ta, one), new Expr.Bin(\"=\", uc, one))); // conjunction\n List<Engine.Table> tables = new ArrayList<>();\n        int n = domain.size();\n        // all subsets of size 0..3 encoded as bitmasks\n        for (int mask = 0; mask < (1 << n); mask++) {\n            if (Integer.bitCount(mask) > 3) continue;\n            List<Object[]> rows = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) if ((mask & (1 << i)) != 0) rows.add(domain.get(i));\n            tables.add(new Engine.Table(List.of(\"X\", \"Y\"), rows));\n        }\n        return tables;\n    }\n\n    static Plan buildPlan(Expr pred, Plan.JoinType type) {\n        // Duplicate column }\n\n    static String canonical(Engine.Output out) {\n        TreeSet<String> rows = new TreeSet<>();\n        for (List<Object> r : out.rows()) rows.add(String.valueOf(r));\n        return DIFFERENT.\n\nRight — the correct transformation for \"WHERE right-col predicate\" over LEFT JOIN is to convert the LEFT JOIN to an INNER JOIN (when predicate is null-rejecting), not to push the filter below the join. The well-known rule: a null-rejecting predicate on the null-supplying side of an outer join effectively turns the outer join into an inner join. So the rewrite is: Filter(p, LeftJoin(T,U)) → Filter(p, InnerJoin(T, U-filtered))? Or simply → InnerJoin(T, U) with p pushed into U and kept... com.pushdown.expr.Expr.Col;\nimport com.pushdown.expr.Expr.IsNotNull;\nimport com.pushdown.expr.Expr.IsNull;\nimport com.pushdown.expr.Expr.Lit;\nimport com.pushdown.expr.Expr.Not;\nimport com.pushdown.expr.Expr.Or;\nimport com.pushdown.plan.Plan;\nimport com.pushdown.plan.Plan.Filter;\nimport com.pushdown.plan.Plan.Join;\nimport com.pushdown.plan.Plan.JoinType;\nimport com.pushdown.plan.Plan.Project;\nimport com.pushdown.plan.Plan.Scan;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.HashSet;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.Set;\n\n/**\n * Predicate pushdown optimizer.\n *\n * Decisions are driven by two analyses over each conjunct:\n *  - column provenance (which join side the referenced columns come from);\n *  - NULL-rejection (whether the predicate can still be TRUE when the columns\n *    of one side are all NULL).\n *\n * Rules:\n *  - INNER JOIN: single-side predicates push to that side; constant predicates\n *    push to the left side; two-side predicates stay above.\n *  - LEFT JOIN: left-side predicates push left; right-side predicates push\n *    right ONLY IF they are NULL-rejecting on the right columns (otherwise a\n *    preserved, NULL-padded row could be wrongly kept/dropped, changing\n *    outer-join semantics); anything else stays above the join.\n * Every decision, including refusals to push, is recorded as a Reason.\n */\npublic final class Optimizer {\n\n    public record Reason(String rule, String detail) {}\n    public record Result(Plan plan, List<Reason> reasons) {}\n\n    private final Map<String, Engine.Table> catalog;\n    private final List<Reason> reasons = new ArrayList<>();\n\n    private Optimizer(Map<String, Engine.Table> catalog) { this.catalog = catalog; }\n\n    public static Result optimize(Plan p, Map<String, Engine.Table> catalog) {\n        Optimizer o = new Optimizer(catalog);\n        Plan np = o.opt(p);\n        return new Result(np, o.reasons);\n    }\n\n    private Plan opt(Plan p) {\n        return switch (p) {\n            case Scan s -> s;\n            case Filter f -> pushAll(splitAnd(f.pred()), opt(f.child()));\n            case Project pr -> new Project(pr.items(), opt(pr.child()));\n            case Join j -> new Join(j.type(), opt(j.left()), opt(j.right()), j.on());\n        };\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- AND split\n\n    private static List<Expr> splitAnd(Expr e) {\n        List<Expr> out = new ArrayList<>();\n        collectAnd(e, out);\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    private static void collectAnd(Expr e, List<Expr> out) {\n        if (e instanceof And a) {\n            collectAnd(a.l(), out);\n            collectAnd(a.r(), out);\n        } else {\n            out.add(e);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------- pushdown\n\n    private Plan pushAll(List<Expr> conjuncts, Plan child) {\n        Plan cur = child;\n        List<Expr> remaining = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Expr c : conjuncts) {\n            Expr folded = foldConstant(c);\n            if (folded instanceof Lit l && Boolean.TRUE.equals(l.value())) {\n                reasons.add(new Reason(\"constant-true-dropped\",\n                        \"predicate is constant TRUE, dropped: \" + show(c)));\n                continue;\n            }\n            Placement pl = place(folded, cur);\n            cur = pl.plan();\n            if (!pl.placed()) remaining.add(folded);\n        }\n        for (Expr r : remaining) cur = new Filter(r, cur);\n        return cur;\n    }\n\n    private record Placement(Plan plan, boolean placed) {}\n\n    /** Try to push {@code pred} as deep as possible below {@code node}. */\n    private Placement place(Expr pred, Plan node) {\n        return switch (node) {\n            case Join j -> placeIntoJoin(pred, j);\n            case Filter f -> {\n                Placement inner = place(pred, f.child());\n                yield new Placement(new Filter(f.pred(), inner.plan()), inner.placed());\n            }\n            case Project pr -> {\n                reasons.add(new Reason(\"kept-above-project\",\n                        \"cannot push predicate through projection: \" + show(pred)));\n                yield new Placement(node, false);\n            }\n            case Scan s -> new Placement(new Filter(pred, s), true);\n        };\n    }\n\n    private Placement placeIntoJoin(Expr pred, Join j) {\n        Set<Col> cols = Expr.columnsOf(pred);\n        Set<Col> leftCols = new HashSet<>(Engine.schemaOf(j.left(), catalog));\n        Set<Col> rightCols = new HashSet<>(Engine.schemaOf(j.right(), catalog));\n        boolean refL = intersects(cols, leftCols);\n        boolean refR = intersects(cols, rightCols);\n\n        if (refL && refR) {\n            reasons.add(new Reason(\"kept-above-join\",\n                    \"predicate references both join sides, kept above \" + j.type() + \" join: \" + show(pred)));\n            return new Placement(j, false);\n        }\n\n        if (!refL && !refR) {\n            // Constant predicate (e.g. FALSE after folding).\n            if (j.type() == JoinType.LEFT) {\n                reasons.add(new Reason(\"constant-kept-above-left-join\",\n                        \"constant predicate kept above LEFT JOIN: pushing it into the right input \"\n                                + \"would turn preserved rows into filtered ones and vice versa: \" + show(pred)));\n                return new Placement(j, false);\n            }\n            reasons.add(new Reason(\"constant-pushed-inner\",\n                    \"constant predicate pushed to the left input of INNER JOIN \"\n                            + \"(empty left side empties the join, same as filtering above): \" + show(pred)));\n            return new Placement(new Join(j.type(), new Filter(pred, j.left()), j.right(), j.on()), true);\n        }\n\n        if (refL) {\n            Placement inner = place(pred, j.left());\n            reasons.add(new Reason(\"push-left\",\n                    \"predicate references only left-side columns, pushed below \" + j.type() + \" join: \" + show(pred)));\n            return new Placement(new Join(j.type(), inner.plan(), j.right(), j.on()), true);\n        }\n\n        // Right-side only.\n        if (j.type() == JoinType.INNER) {\n            Placement inner = place(pred, j.right());\n            reasons.add(new Reason(\"push-right-inner\",\n                    \"right-side predicate pushed below INNER JOIN: \" + show(pred)));\n            return new Placement(new Join(j.type(), j.left(), inner.plan(), j.on()), true);\n        }\n        if (nullRejecting(pred, rightCols)) {\n            Placement inner = place(pred, j.right());\n            reasons.add(new Reason(\"push-right-null-rejecting\",\n                    \"right-side predicate is NULL-rejecting on the right input, so preserved NULL-padded \"\n                            + \"rows can never satisfy it; safe to push below LEFT JOIN: \" + show(pred)));\n            return new Placement(new Join(j.type(), j.left(), inner.plan(), j.on()), true);\n        }\n        reasons.add(new Reason(\"kept-for-null-preservation\",\n                \"right-side predicate is NOT NULL-rejecting; pushing it below the LEFT JOIN could drop \"\n                        + \"or fabricate preserved rows, so it stays above the join: \" + show(pred)));\n        return new Placement(j, false);\n    }\n\n    private static boolean intersects(Set<Col> a, Set<Col> b) {\n        return !Collections.disjoint(a, b);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------- NULL-rejection\n\n    /**\n     * Conservative static NULL-rejection test: returns true only if the\n     * predicate can never evaluate to TRUE when every column in {@code cols}\n     * is NULL.\n     */\n    public static boolean nullRejecting(Expr p, Set<Col> cols) {\n        return switch (p) {\n            case Lit l -> !Boolean.TRUE.equals(l.value());       // constant FALSE/NULL rejects everything\n            case Col c -> cols.contains(c);                       // NULL in boolean position -> UNKNOWN\n            case Bin b -> !Collections.disjoint(Expr.columnsOf(b), cols); // NULL operand -> UNKNOWN/NULL\n            case And a -> nullRejecting(a.l(), cols) || nullRejecting(a.r(), cols);\n            case Or o -> nullRejecting(o.l(), cols) && nullRejecting(o.r(), cols);\n            case IsNull i -> false;                               // TRUE exactly when the value IS NULL\n            case IsNotNull i -> !Collections.disjoint(Expr.columnsOf(i.e()), cols);\n            case Not n -> n.e() instanceof IsNull i\n                    && !Collections.disjoint(Expr.columnsOf(i.e()), cols);\n        };\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------- constant fold\n\n    private Expr foldConstant(Expr e) {\n        if (e instanceof Lit) return e;\n        if (!Expr.columnsOf(e).isEmpty()) return e;\n        Object v = Expr.eval(e, List.of(), new Object[0]);\n        reasons.add(new Reason(\"constant-fold\",\n                \"predicate has no column references, folded to \" + v + \": \" + show(e)));\n        return new Lit(v);\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------- display\n\n    public static String show(Expr e) {\n        return switch (e) {\n            case Lit l -> l.value() == null ? \"NULL\" : String.valueOf(l.value());\n            case Col c -> c.table() + \".\" + c.name();\n            case Bin b -> \"(\" + show(b.l()) + \" \" + b.op() + \" \" + show(b.r()) + \")\";\n            case And a -> \"(\" + show(a.l()) + \" AND \" + show(a.r()) + \")\";\n            case Or o -> \"(\" + show(o.l()) + \" OR \" + show(o.r()) + \")\";\n            case Not n -> \"(NOT \" + show(n.e()) + \")\";\n            case IsNull i -> \"(\" + show(i.e()) + \" IS NULL)\";\n            case IsNotNull i -> \"(\" + show(i.e()) + \" IS NOT NULL)\";\n        };\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":165,"oldLines":9,"newStart":165,"newLines":12,"lines":["         if (nullRejecting(pred, rightCols)) {","             Placement inner = place(pred, 表达式谓词下推（Expression Predicate Pushdown）\n\n纯后端、零第三方依赖的 Java 单机内存查询引擎。核心运算（过滤、投影、内连接、左连接、三值逻辑谓词求值）全部自行实现，不调用任何现成 SQL 引擎。提供 JSON-over-HTTP 请求入口，支持执行计划与结果数据导出，并对每个谓词下推决策输出改写理由。\n\n## 构建与运行\n\n需要 JDK 17+（开发验证使用 OpenJDK 21）。\n\n```bash\n./build.sh                 # 编译到 out/\n./run_tests.sh             # 编译并运行全部自动化测试（含穷举验收）\njava -cp out com.pushdown.api.Server 8080   # 启动 HTTP 服务（默认端口 8080）\n```\n\n## 体系结构\n\n```\nsrc/main/java/com/pushdown/\n├── json/Json.java        最小 JSON 解析/序列化（无外部依赖）\n├── expr/Expr.java        表达式树：字面量/列引用/比较/算术/AND/OR/NOT/IS NULL，\n│                         SQL 三值逻辑求值（TRUE/FALSE/UNKNOWN）\n├── plan/Plan.java        计划树：Scan / Filter / Project / Join(INNER, LEFT)\n├── exec/Engine.java      内存执行器：过滤、投影、嵌套循环内连接与左连接\n├── opt/Optimizer.java    谓词下推优化器（核心），输出每条改写理由\n└── api/                  JSON 计划互转 + JDK 内置 HttpServer 入口\nsrc/test/java/com/pushdown/TestRunner.java   测试入口（无测试框架，失败即非零退出）\n```\n\n## 下推规则\n\n优化器对每个合取项（AND 拆分后）做两项分析：\n\n- **列来源（provenance）**：谓词引用的列属于连接的哪一侧；\n- **NULL 拒绝性（null-rejecting）**：当某侧列全为 NULL 时，谓词是否仍可能为 TRUE（保守静态分析，见下表）。\n\n| 场景 | 决策 |\n|---|---|\n| INNER JOIN，只引用左/右单侧 | 下推到对应侧 |\n| INNER JOIN，常量谓词（含 `1=0` 折叠后的 FALSE） | 下推到左输入（左侧为空则连接为空，与上层过滤等价） |\n| LEFT JOIN，只引用左侧 | 下推到左输入 |\n| LEFT JOIN，只引用右侧且 NULL 拒绝 | **LEFT JOIN 退化为 INNER JOIN**，谓词推入右输入 |\n| LEFT JOIN，只引用右侧但非 NULL 拒绝（如 `U.C IS NULL`） | 保留在连接之上，理由 `kept-for-null-preservation` |\n| LEFT JOIN，常量谓词 | 保留在连接之上（推入任一侧都会改变保留行语义） |\n| 引用双侧 | 保留在连接之上 |\n| 穿越 Project | 不下推 |\n\nNULL 拒绝性判定（保守）：比较/算术运算引用了该侧列 → 拒绝；`IS NOT NULL` → 拒绝；`IS NULL` → 不拒绝；`AND` 任一拒绝则拒绝；`OR` 两侧都拒绝才拒绝；常量 FALSE → （空真）拒绝，常量 TRUE → 不拒绝。\n\n**关键正确性点**：对 LEFT JOIN 右表的 NULL 拒绝谓词，不能简单把 Filter 推到右表下方而保留 LEFT JOIN——那样未匹配的左行会以全 NULL 保留行的形式绕过过滤凭空出现。正确改写是教科书规则：NULL 拒绝谓词使外连接**退化为内连接**（`Filter(p, T⋉U)` ≡ `T⋈Filter(p, U)`，p 为右表 NULL 拒绝谓词）。本项目初版实现曾犯此错误，被穷举验收测试捕获后修正（见\"验证记录\"）。\n\n## JSON 请求格式\n\n`POST /query`：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"tables\": {\"T\": {\"columns\": [\"A\",\"B\"], \"rows\": [[1,1],[2,null]]}},\n  \"plan\":   {\"type\": \"filter\", \"pred\": {...}, \"child\": {...}},\n  \"optimize\": true,\n  \"exportDir\": \"out/export-demo\"\n}\n```\n\n- 表达式：`{\"lit\": v}`、`{\"col\": \"T.A\"}`（必须限定表名，支持重名列）、`{\"op\": \"=|!=|<|<=|>|>=|+|-|*\", \"args\": [l,r]}`、`{\"op\":\"and|or\",\"args\":[...]}`、`{\"op\":\"not|isnull|isnotnull\",\"args\":[e]}`\n- 计划节点：`scan` / `filter` / `project` / `join`（`joinType`: `inner`|`left`）\n- `optimize` 缺省为 `true`；`exportDir` 可选，指定后把执行计划写入 `plan.json`、结果数据写入 `data.json`（即\"数据与执行计划可导出\"）\n\n响应：`originalPlan`、`optimizedPlan`、`reasons`（每条含 `rule` 与 `detail`）、`schema`、`rows`。\n\n## 请求样例\n\n`samples/` 下四个样例，启动服务后可这样执行：\n\n```bash\njava -cp out com.pushdown.api.Server 18080 &\ncurl -s -X POST -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\\n     --data @samples/request1_left_null_rejecting.json http://127.0.0.1:18080/query\n```\n\n1. `request1_left_null_rejecting.json` — LEFT JOIN + 右表 NULL 拒绝谓词 `U.C=10`：LEFT 退化为 INNER，谓词推入右表；\n2. `request2_left_isnull_kept.json` — LEFT JOIN + `U.C IS NULL`：非 NULL 拒绝，保留在连接之上；\n3. `request3_constant_false.json` — INNER JOIN + 常量假 `1=0`：先折叠为 `false`，再推入左输入，结果为空；\n4. `request4_and_export.json` — AND 拆分后双侧分别下推，并把计划与结果导出到 `out/export-demo/`。\n\n## 验证记录（实际运行，如实记录）\n\n环境：OpenJDK 21.0.12.1，Ubuntu（Linux 6.8）。\n\n### 自动化测试\n\n命令：`./run_tests.sh`\n\n最终结果：\n\n```\nbuild ok\nexhaustive: 16900 table-pairs x 2 join types x 10 predicates = 338000 comparisons, mismatches=0\npassed=37 failed=0\nALL TESTS PASSED\n```\n\n穷举验收方法：域 `{NULL,1,2}`，两列行共 9 种，枚举所有 0~3 行的表（130 张），两两组合（16900 对）× 2 种连接 × 10 个谓词（覆盖右表 NULL 过滤 `U.C=1`/`U.C IS NULL`/`IS NOT NULL`、重名列 `T.B`/`U.B`、常量假 `false` 与 `1=0`、双侧引用、AND/OR 组合），逐组比较改写前后结果（行多重集），338000 组全部一致。\n\n**开发过程中被发现并修复的问题（如实记录）**：初版把 LEFT JOIN 右表的 NULL 拒绝谓词直接推到右表下方且保留 LEFT JOIN，穷举测试报出 40882 组失配（如 `T=[(NULL,NULL)], U=[]` 时改写后多出一行全 NULL 保留行）。随后改为正确规则\"NULL 拒绝谓词使 LEFT JOIN 退化为 INNER JOIN\"，复测 0 失配。除此之外无其他未通过项。\n\n### HTTP 样例（实际输出摘要）\n\n- 样例 1：`optimizedPlan` 中 `joinType` 变为 `inner`，右输入套上 `filter U.C=10`；`reasons` 含 `push-right-null-rejecting`；结果 `[[1,1,1,10]]`。\n- 样例 2：计划未改动；`reasons` 含 `kept-for-null-preservation`；结果 `[[2,2,2,null],[3,null,null,null]]`（保留行正确参与 `IS NULL` 过滤）。\n- 样例 3：`reasons` 依次含 `constant-fold`、`constant-pushed-inner`；结果 `[]`。\n- 样例 4：AND 两项分别下推左右两侧；`out/export-demo/plan.json` 与 `data.json` 成功写出。\n\n全部测试通过，无遗留未通过项。\n"},"name":"Write","type":"tool_use"}],"id":"msg_5ce1a67e22ad2a55ed63f9fa","model":"auto_model/urm","role":"assistant","stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"type":"message","usage":{"input_tokens":1831,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":65536,"output_tokens":2207,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"},"stop_details":null},"wireToolInputs":{"call_e8092405f6f64b4abfb9a6f2":{"content":"# 表达式谓词下推（Expression Predicate Pushdown）\n\n纯后端、零第三方依赖的 Java 单机内存查询引擎。核心运算（过滤、投影、内连接、左连接、三值逻辑谓词求值）全部自行实现，不调用任何现成 SQL 引擎。提供 JSON-over-HTTP 请求入口，支持执行计划与结果数据导出，并对每个谓词下推决策输出改写理由。\n\n## 构建与运行\n\n需要 JDK 17+（开发验证使用 OpenJDK 21）。\n\n```bash\n./build.sh                 # 编译到 out/\n./run_tests.sh             # 编译并运行全部自动化测试（含穷举验收）\njava -cp out com.pushdown.api.Server 8080   # 启动 HTTP 服务（默认端口 8080）\n```\n\n## 体系结构\n\n```\nsrc/main/java/com/pushdown/\n├── json/Json.java        最小 JSON 解析/序列化（无外部依赖）\n├── expr/Expr.java        表达式树：字面量/列引用/比较/算术/AND/OR/NOT/IS NULL，\n│                         SQL 三值逻辑求值（TRUE/FALSE/UNKNOWN）\n├── plan/Plan.java        计划树：Scan / Filter / Project / Join(INNER, LEFT)\n├── exec/Engine.java      内存执行器：过滤、投影、嵌套循环内连接与左连接\n├── opt/Optimizer.java    谓词下推优化器（核心），输出每条改写理由\n└── api/                  JSON 计划互转 + JDK 内置 HttpServer 入口\nsrc/test/java/com/pushdown/TestRunner.java   测试入口（无测试框架，失败即非零退出）\n```\n\n## 下推规则\n\n优化器对每个合取项（AND 拆分后）做两项分析：\n\n- **列来源（provenance）**：谓词引用的列属于连接的哪一侧；\n- **NULL 拒绝性（null-rejecting）**：当某侧列全为 NULL 时，谓词是否仍可能为 TRUE（保守静态分析，见下表）。\n\n| 场景 | 决策 |\n|---|---|\n| INNER JOIN，只引用左/右单侧 | 下推到对应侧 |\n| INNER JOIN，常量谓词（含 `1=0` 折叠后的 FALSE） | 下推到左输入（左侧为空则连接为空，与上层过滤等价） |\n| LEFT JOIN，只引用左侧 | 下推到左输入 |\n| LEFT JOIN，只引用右侧且 NULL 拒绝 | **LEFT JOIN 退化为 INNER JOIN**，谓词推入右输入 |\n| LEFT JOIN，只引用右侧但非 NULL 拒绝（如 `U.C IS NULL`） | 保留在连接之上，理由 `kept-for-null-preservation` |\n| LEFT JOIN，常量谓词 | 保留在连接之上（推入任一侧都会改变保留行语义） |\n| 引用双侧 | 保留在连接之上 |\n| 穿越 Project | 不下推 |\n\nNULL 拒绝性判定（保守）：比较/算术运算引用了该侧列 → 拒绝；`IS NOT NULL` → 拒绝；`IS NULL` → 不拒绝；`AND` 任一拒绝则拒绝；`OR` 两侧都拒绝才拒绝；常量 FALSE → （空真）拒绝，常量 TRUE → 不拒绝。\n\n**关键正确性点**：对 LEFT JOIN 右表的 NULL 拒绝谓词，不能简单把 Filter 推到右表下方而保留 LEFT JOIN——那样未匹配的左行会以全 NULL 保留行的形式绕过过滤凭空出现。正确改写是教科书规则：NULL 拒绝谓词使外连接**退化为内连接**（`Filter(p, T⋉U)` ≡ `T⋈Filter(p, U)`，p 为右表 NULL 拒绝谓词）。本项目初版实现曾犯此错误，被穷举验收测试捕获后修正（见\"验证记录\"）。\n\n## JSON 请求格式\n\n`POST /query`：\n\n```json\n{\n  \"tables\": {\"T\": {\"columns\": [\"A\",\"B\"], \"rows\": [[1,1],[2,null]]}},\n  \"plan\":   {\"type\": \"filter\", \"pred\": {...}, \"child\": {...}},\n  \"optimize\": true,\n  \"exportDir\": \"out/export-demo\"\n}\n```\n\n- 表达式：`{\"lit\": v}`、`{\"col\": \"T.A\"}`（必须限定表名，支持重名列）、`{\"op\": \"=|!=|<|<=|>|>=|+|-|*\", \"args\": [l,r]}`、`{\"op\":\"and|or\",\"args\":[...]}`、`{\"op\":\"not|isnull|isnotnull\",\"args\":[e]}`\n- 计划节点：`scan` / `filter` / `project` / `join`（`joinType`: `inner`|`left`）\n- `optimize` 缺省为 `true`；`exportDir` 可选，指定后把执行计划写入 `plan.json`、结果数据写入 `data.json`（即\"数据与执行计划可导出\"）\n\n响应：`originalPlan`、`optimizedPlan`、`reasons`（每条含 `rule` 与 `detail`）、`schema`、`rows`。\n\n## 请求样例\n\n`samples/` 下四个样例，启动服务后可这样执行：\n\n```bash\njava -cp out com.pushdown.api.Server 18080 &\ncurl -s -X POST -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\\n     --data @samples/request1_left_null_rejecting.json http://127.0.0.1:18080/query\n```\n\n1. `request1_left_null_rejecting.json` — LEFT JOIN + 右表 NULL 拒绝谓词 `U.C=10`：LEFT 退化为 INNER，谓词推入右表；\n2. `request2_left_isnull_kept.json` — LEFT JOIN + `U.C IS NULL`：非 NULL 拒绝，保留在连接之上；\n3. `request3_constant_false.json` — INNER JOIN + 常量假 `1=0`：先折叠为 `false`，再推入左输入，结果为空；\n4. `request4_and_export.json` — AND 拆分后双侧分别下推，并把计划与结果导出到 `out/export-demo/`。\n\n## 验证记录（实际运行，如实记录）\n\n环境：OpenJDK 21.0.12.1，Ubuntu（Linux 6.8）。\n\n### 自动化测试\n\n命令：`./run_tests.sh`\n\n最终结果：\n\n```\nbuild ok\nexhaustive: 16900 table-pairs x 2 join types x 10 predicates = 338000 comparisons, mismatches=0\npassed=37 failed=0\nALL TESTS PASSED\n```\n\n穷举验收方法：域 `{NULL,1,2}`，两列行共 9 种，枚举所有 0~3 行的表（130 张），两两组合（16900 对）× 2 种连接 × 10 个谓词（覆盖右表 NULL 过滤 `U.C=1`/`U.C IS NULL`/`IS NOT NULL`、重名列 `T.B`/`U.B`、常量假 `false` 与 `1=0`、双侧引用、AND/OR 组合），逐组比较改写前后结果（行多重集），338000 组全部一致。\n\n**开发过程中被发现并修复的问题（如实记录）**：初版把 LEFT JOIN 右表的 NULL 拒绝谓词直接推到右表下方且保留 LEFT JOIN，穷举测试报出 40882 组失配（如 `T=[(NULL,NULL)], U=[]` 时改写后多出一行全 NULL 保留行）。随后改为正确规则\"NULL 拒绝谓词使 LEFT JOIN 退化为 INNER JOIN\"，复测 0 失配。除此之外无其他未通过项。\n\n### HTTP 样例（实际输出摘要）\n\n- 样例 1：`optimizedPlan` 中 `joinType` 变为 `inner`，右输入套上 `filter U.C=10`；`reasons` 含 `push-right-null-rejecting`；结果 `[[1,1,1,10]]`。\n- 样例 2：计划未改动；`reasons` 含 `kept-for-null-preservation`；结果 `[[2,2,2,null],[3,null,null,null]]`（保留行正确参与 `IS NULL` 过滤）。\n- 样例 3：`reasons` 依次含 `constant-fold`、`constant-pushed-inner`；结果 `[]`。\n- 样例 4：AND 两项分别下推左右两侧；`out/export-demo/plan.json` 与 `data.json` 成功写出。\n\n全部测试通过，无遗留未通过项。\n","file_path":"/home/admin/Downloads/biaozhul/opp14/b/README.md"}},"apiBlockIndex":2,"type":"assistant","uuid":"0824277b-78f8-42ab-9567-0dbbf0b6d848","timestamp":"2026-09-23T02:58:52.368Z","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":null,"session_id":"eb7b3e82-232b-4ef9-84e5-467193bf19b0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/admin/Downloads/biaozhul/opp14/b","sessionId":"eb7b3e82-232b-4ef9-84e5-467193bf19b0","version":"2.1.280","gitBranch":"ab/run-20260922T151547Z/opp14-b","slug":"async-wibbling-hamster"}
